Catalogs
Reference/Catalogs

Ability catalog

Filter by name, class, specialization or targeting rule.

330 inherited named abilities with revised unlocks, prototype timing/cost profiles and explicit review status.

A profile describes the primary operation. It does not silently implement every named secondary effect. Read the class page and execution contract before enabling an ability.

Searchable ability definitions

Ability / unlock Targeting Intent and numerical profile Timing / cost
